<analysis>**original_problem_statement:**
The user wants to build a unified, self-hosted media pipeline called WatchNexus. This system should replace the need for multiple applications like Sonarr, Radarr, Prowlarr, qBittorrent, Bazarr, and Jellyfin. The goal is a single, **fully self-contained application** that handles requesting, acquiring, organizing, and watching media.

**New User Requests from this Session:**
1.  Fix a bug where deleting users fails.

5.  Create release packages () for Linux and Windows that can be run standalone, without requiring the user to have Node.js installed.

**what currently exists?**
The application is a full-stack media server named WatchNexus with a FastAPI backend and React frontend.

The agent has just completed a deep functional code audit, finding and fixing several critical issues. Key settings (IPTV, sidebar visibility, theme, download client) have been migrated from browser  to backend database persistence, ensuring they sync across devices and users. A major bug preventing user deletion due to foreign key constraints has been resolved.

The agent also created standalone release packages () for Linux and Windows. These packages include a pre-built frontend and startup scripts, allowing the app to run without external dependencies like Node.js.

The most recent fix addressed a UI bug in the file browser where navigating folders required a double-click; this has been changed to a more intuitive single-click.

**Code Architecture**
The project's source code is duplicated. The application runs from  and , while the canonical code is maintained in  and . Changes must be synced from the running directories to the separated ones. For standalone releases, the backend is configured to serve a pre-built React app from .


**Key Technical Concepts**
-   **Settings Persistence:** Migrating settings from  to backend APIs and a SQLite database ( table) to enable multi-device and multi-user sync.
-   **Foreign Key Constraints:** Fixing  operations by ensuring all dependent records in related tables are removed before the primary record to prevent database errors.
-   **Standalone App Deployment:** Building the React frontend (yarn run v1.22.22
info Visit https://yarnpkg.com/en/docs/cli/run for documentation about this command.) and configuring the FastAPI backend () to serve the static assets, removing the need for a separate Node.js server in production/release.
